# Beutekugel all-level blueprint audit - 2026-09-19

Current implementation: see [beutekugel_blueprints_script_fix_changelog.md](beutekugel_blueprints_script_fix_changelog.md). The DBR-only blueprint roll described below failed in game and was removed from the 12 active sphere loot records. Blueprint delivery now occurs in the sphere-opening script. This document remains as a historical audit of the first attempt.

Follow-up correction: see [beutekugel_blueprints_drop_fix_changelog.md](beutekugel_blueprints_drop_fix_changelog.md). The initial 35 pools lacked a positive plain-item outcome weight; this was added after a report of missing drops. Original pool hashes below refer to the pre-correction version. In-game confirmation remains pending.

12 Beutekugel variants now reserve one additional first drop exclusively for the shared blueprint pool. 948 unique blueprint records have equal table-selection weight (1/948) at every character level. Sources were freshly extracted from the installed base, GDX1, GDX2 and GDX3 databases, overlaid in that order, then by mod overrides.

The first-drop weights are 100 for the blueprint group and zero for every other group. Existing drop-position arrays are shifted right; both spawn-count equations increase by one. Magical spheres also use the shared pool for their existing extra blueprint rolls. Other loot groups retain their previous items and weights. Blueprint records, crafting requirements, enemy records and shared vanilla loot tables are not edited.

Fixed-weight tables bypass both LevelTable selection and dynamic item-level weighting. Parent weights equal descendant counts, avoiding bias from a smaller final leaf. The installed template limit is 30 entries per table. Valid unnamed ItemArtifactFormula recipes (including awakened crafting recipes) are included; description tags are not required. All potion OneShot_SkillUnlock blueprints are included. Sandbox prototypes, developer test blueprints and non-blueprint internal ascension/reroll/transmutation operations are excluded.

This guarantees a blueprint selection in the DBR loot configuration, not an unlearned recipe. Engine handling of learned recipes, multiplayer ownership and visible drops requires in-game verification. No game session or compilation was performed. Compile manually in Asset Manager and test level 1 and level 100 characters, including a character with known recipes.

Loot-position semantics reference: [Crate developer explanation](https://forums.crateentertainment.com/t/question-on-modifying-loottables/33001/7).

## Coverage by effective source

- base: 316
- gdx1: 301
- gdx2: 133
- gdx3: 198

## Supporting files and verification

- Independent post-write graph audit passed: 35 reachable pool tables, 948 unique blueprint leaves, no cycles, no duplicate leaves, no gaps and no table exceeding 30 entries. Every leaf has probability 1/948 (about 0.105485%); summed probability is 1 within floating-point tolerance. Includes 32 potion-unlock blueprints.
- Independent backup comparison passed for all 12 sphere records: exclusively blueprint first position; original Chance arrays preserved exactly after the prepended position (apart from the previously unused blueprint group); spawn-count expressions each increased by one; unrelated fields unchanged. All 47 output SHA-256 hashes matched the manifest.
- tools/apply_beutekugel_blueprints.ps1: retained generator; default is dry-run, -Apply writes once and refuses repeat application.
- audit/beutekugel_blueprints_2026-09-19/before/: byte-preserving backups of the 12 modified loot DBRs, at their original relative record paths.
- audit/beutekugel_blueprints_2026-09-19/manifest.json: complete blueprint inventory, exclusions, source database hashes and output hashes.
- beutekugel_blueprints_changelog.md: this complete field-level audit.
- Preflight: all pool references resolve; all blueprint crafting outputs and potion unlock skills resolve; all 12 containers use the expected loot record with lootChance=100; exclusive first-drop selection verified; all written DBRs round-trip exactly.
- No existing DBRs other than the 12 sphere loot files were changed. No files deleted. Compilation deliberately not performed; compile the mod manually.
